Business Overview                                                 業務概覽

December 31, 2022 (the “Reporting Period”) we have                （「 報 告 期 」）， 儘 管 面 臨 持 續 的 市 場 挑 戰 及 不 確 定
achieved strong results and further expanded our overall          性，我們取得了出色的業績，並進一步擴大了我
competitive advantages, despite ongoing market challenges         們的整體競爭優勢。本集團的收入由2021 年的
and uncertainties. The Group’s revenue grew by 13.2%              1,757.8 百萬美元增長 13.2% 至 2022 年的 1,989.3
from US$1,757.8 million in 2021 to US$1,989.3 million in          百萬美元，顯著優於市場水平，並進一步鞏固了我
2022, significantly outperforming the market and further          們的市場領導地位。同年，我們的原始品牌製造
strengthening our industry leadership position. During the        （「OBM」）業務增長22.1%。本集團的收入以當地貨
same year, our Original Brand Manufacturing (“OBM”)               幣計算則增長15.1%。我們的銷售額繼於2018年至

 CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                      企業管治常規
 The Company strives to attain and maintain high standards           本公司相信有效的企業管治架構是提高及保障股東
 of corporate governance best suited to the needs and                及其他持份者權益與提升股東價值的基本要素，因
 interests of the Group as it believes that an effective corporate   此致力達致及維持最符合本集團需要及利益的高水
 governance framework is fundamental to promoting and                平企業管治。因此，本公司已採納及應用企業管治
 safeguarding interests of Shareholders and other stakeholders       原則及常規，強調具備一個優秀的董事會、有效的
 and enhancing Shareholder value. Accordingly, the Company           風險管理及內部控制系統、嚴格的披露常規、透明
 has adopted and applied corporate governance principles             度及問責性，以及與股東及其他持份者進行有效的
 and practices that emphasize a quality board of Directors,          溝通及互動。此外，本集團致力不斷提升該等標準
 effective risk management and internal control systems,             及常規，並建立穩健的合規及道德管治文化，以配

the Company, which is underpinned by the core values of            定本集團的宗旨、價值觀及策略方向，以及培育具
acting lawfully, ethically and responsibly across all levels       前瞻性、擁抱變化及著重競爭力的文化方面發揮領
of the Group. The Board plays a leading role in defining           導作用。本集團在營運常規、工作場所政策及常規
the purpose, values and strategic direction of the Group           以及與持份者的關係中建立及貫徹反映所需文化。

embracing and competitiveness-focused. The desired culture         工具，包括員工參與、員工留任及培訓、嚴格的財
is developed and reflected consistently in the operating           務報告、有效及可用的舉報框架、法律及監管合規
practices of the Group, workplace policies and practices as        （包括遵守行為守則及集團政策）以及員工安全、福
well as relations with stakeholders. The Board’s oversight of      祉及支持。經考慮一系列情況下的企業文化，董事

Responsibilities                                                   職責

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                               主席及行政總裁

Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code requires that the roles of          企業管治守則第C.2.1條要求主席與行政總裁的角色
chairman and chief executive officer be separate and not be        應有區分，並不應由一人同時兼任。
performed by the same individual.


However, the Company does not have separate chairman               然而，本公司並無區分主席及行政總裁，而主席及
and chief executive officer roles, and the responsibilities of     行政總裁的職責均由潘龍泉先生承擔。董事會相
both chairman and chief executive officer vest in Mr. Pan          信，由同一人兼任主席及行政總裁的職責，可確保
Longquan. The Board believes that vesting the responsibilities     本集團內部領導貫徹一致，使本集團的整體策略規
of both chairman and chief executive officer in the same           劃更有效及更具效率。此外，董事會合共七名董事
person has the benefit of ensuring the consistent leadership       中有三名獨立非執行董事，董事會內將有足夠的獨
within the Group and enables more effective and efficient          立意見，以保障本公司及股東的整體利益。
overall strategic planning of the Group. Besides, with three
independent non-executive Directors out of a total of seven
Directors on the Board, there will be sufficient independent
voice within the Board to protect the interests of the Company
and the Shareholders as a whole.

Therefore, the Board considers that the balance of power and         因此，董事會認為，現行安排不會損害權力與權限
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and       之間的平衡，而此架構將使本公司能夠迅速及有效
this structure will enable the Company to make and implement         地作出及實施決策。董事會將繼續進行檢討，並會
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue          在計及本集團整體情況後考慮於適當時候將董事會
to review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the        主席與本公司行政總裁的角色分開。
Board and chief executive officer of the Company at a time

(b)   Basis of preparation of the financial statements              (b)    財務報表編製基準

      The consolidated financial statements of the Group                   本集團截至2022 年12 月31 日止年度的綜合
      for the year ended December 31, 2022 comprise the                    財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司以及本集
      Company and its subsidiaries and the Group’s interest                團於聯營公司及合營企業的權益。
      in associates and a joint venture.


      The measurement basis used in the preparation of the                 編製綜合財務報表時使用的計量基準為歷史
      consolidated financial statements is the historical cost             成本法，但以下資產及負債按下文會計政策
      basis except that the following assets and liabilities are           中解釋的公允價值列示。
      stated at their fair value as explained in the accounting
      policies as set out below.


      The preparation of financial statements in conformity                編製符合香港財務報告準則之財務報表要求
      with HKFRSs requires management to make                              管理層須作出判斷、估計及假設，而該等判
      judgements, estimates and assumptions that affect                    斷、估計及假設會影響政策應用以及所呈報
      the application of policies and reported amounts of                  之資產、負債、收入及開支金額。此等估計
      assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates              及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及在有關情況下
      and associated assumptions are based on historical                   被認為屬合理之各項其他因素，而所得結果
      experience and various other factors that are believed               乃用作判斷顯然無法輕易透過其他來源獲得
      to be reasonable under the circumstances, the results                有關資產與負債賬面值之依據。實際結果或
      of which form the basis of making the judgements                     有別於此等估計。
      about carrying values of assets and liabilities that are
      not readily apparent from other sources. Actual results
      may differ from these estimates.


      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed                此等估計及相關假設會持續審核。若會計估
      on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates               計的修訂僅影響作出修訂的期間，將會於該
      are recognized in the period in which the estimate is                期間確認，若修訂影響當期及未來期間，則
      revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the          會於作出修訂及其後期間確認。
      period of the revision and future periods if the revision
      affects both current and future periods.


      Judgements made by management in the application                     附註 3 討論了管理層在應用香港財務報告準
      of HKFRSs that have significant effect on the financial              則時作出的對財務報表有重大影響的判斷以
      statements and major sources of estimation uncertainty               及估計不確定性的主要來源。
      are discussed in Note 3.

 (d)      Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                     (d)   附屬公司及非控股權益

          Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The               附屬公司乃由本集團所控制的實體。當本集
          Group controls an entity when it is exposed, or has                  團能透過其參與承擔或享有實體可變回報的
          rights, to variable returns from its involvement with the            風險或權利，並能夠向實體使用其權力影響
          entity and has the ability to affect those returns through           回報金額，即對該實體擁有控制權。評估本
          its power over the entity. When assessing whether the                集團是否擁有權力時僅考慮本集團及其他人
          Group has power, only substantive rights (held by the                士所持有的實質權利。
          Group and other parties) are considered.


          An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into the               於附屬公司的投資自控制開始日直至控制終
          consolidated financial statements from the date that                 止日併入綜合財務報表。集團內部往來的結
          control commences until the date that control ceases.                餘、交易及現金流量及由內部交易產生的未
          Intra-group balances and transactions and cash flows                 變現利潤，均在編製綜合財務報表時全數抵
          and any unrealized profits arising from intra-group                  銷。集團內部交易所產生的未變現虧損的抵
          transactions are eliminated in full in preparing the                 銷方法與未變現收益相同，但只限於沒有減
          consolidated financial statements. Unrealized losses                 值證據的情況下。
          resulting from intra-group transactions are eliminated in
          the same way as unrealized gains but only to the extent
          that there is no evidence of impairment.

          included in a disposal group that is classified as held            最初按成本記錄的投資就本集團分佔被投資
          for sale). Under the equity method, the investment is              方可識別資產淨值於收購日期的公允價值超
          initially recorded at cost, adjusted for any excess of the         過投資成本的任何部分（如有）作出調整。

          investment (if any). The cost of the investment includes           的任何於聯營公司的直接投資。其後，投資
          purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the           就本集團分佔被投資方資產淨值於收購後的
          acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment           變動及與投資有關的任何減值虧損作出調整
          into the associate that forms part of the Group’s equity           （見附註2(k)(ii)）。於收購日期，本集團分佔
          investment. Thereafter, the investment is adjusted for             被投資方的收購後、除稅後業績以及年內的
          the post acquisition change in the Group’s share of the            任何減值虧損乃於綜合損益表內確認，而本
          investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating             集團分佔被投資方其他全面收益的收購後除
          to the investment (see Note 2(k)(ii)). Any the Group’s             稅後項目於綜合損益及其他全面收益表內確
          share of the post-acquisition, post-tax results of the             認。
          investees and any impairment losses for the year are
          recognized in the consolidated statements of profit or
          loss, whereas the Group’s share of the post-acquisition
          post-tax items of the investees’ other comprehensive

          profit or loss and other comprehensive income.


          When the Group’s share of losses exceeds its interest              當本集團分佔虧損超過其於聯營公司的權益
          in the associate, the Group’s interest is reduced to               時，本集團的權益減少至零且終止確認進一
          nil and recognition of further losses is discontinued              步的虧損，惟本集團已產生法定或推定責任
          except to the extent that the Group has incurred legal             或代表被投資方作出付款則除外。就此而
          or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf             言，本集團的權益為根據權益法計量的投資
          of the investee. For this purpose, the Group’s interest is         的賬面值連同實質上構成本集團於聯營公司
          the carrying amount of the investment under the equity             的投資淨額的一部分的任何其他長期權益。
          method together with any other long-term interests that
          in substance form part of the Group’s net investment in
          the associate.

          between the Group and the associates are eliminated                現損益的對銷限於本集團於被投資方的權

          except where unrealized losses provide evidence of an              憑據則除外，在此情況下，未變現虧損立即
          impairment of the asset transferred, in which case they            於損益中確認。
          are recognized immediately in profit or loss.

      dismantle and remove the underlying asset or to                     惠。使用權資產隨後按成本減去累計折舊及
      restore the underlying asset or the site on which it                減值虧損列賬（見附註2(h)及2(k)(ii)）。
      is located, discounted to their present value, less
      any lease incentives received. The right-of-use asset
      is subsequently stated at cost less accumulated
      depreciation and impairment losses (see Notes 2(h)
      and 2(k)(ii)).


      The lease liability is remeasured when there is a                   當未來租賃付款因某一指數或比率變動而變
      change in future lease payments arising from a change               更，或當本集團預期根據殘值擔保估計預期
      in an index or rate, or there is a change in the Group’s            應付的金額有變，或因重新評估本集團是否
      estimate of the amount expected to be payable under                 合理地確定將行使購買、續租或終止選擇權
      a residual value guarantee, or there is a change arising            而產生變動，則會重新計量租賃負債。按此
      from the reassessment of whether the Group will be                  方式重新計量租賃負債時，使用權資產的賬
      reasonably certain to exercise a purchase, extension                面值將作相應調整，或倘使用權資產的賬面
      or termination option. When the lease liability is                  值已減至零，則於損益內列賬。
      remeasured in this way, a corresponding adjustment is
      made to the carrying amount of the right-of-use asset,
      or is recorded in profit or loss if the carrying amount of
      the right-of-use asset has been reduced to zero.

                    its recoverable amount. Impairment losses                       確認的減值虧損會被分配，以按比例
                    recognized in respect of cash-generating units                  首先減低分配至現金產生單位（或一
                    are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount               組單位）任何商譽的賬面值，隨後減
                    of any goodwill allocated to the cash-generating                低單位（或一組單位）其他資產的賬

                    carrying amount of the other assets in the unit                 低於其個別公允價值減出售成本或使
                    (or group of units) on a pro rata basis, except                 用價值（如可釐定）。

(l)    Inventories                                                  (l)     存貨

       Inventories are assets which are held for sale in                    存貨為在日常業務過程中持作銷售、處於為
       the ordinary course of business, in the process of                   這些銷售的生產過程中，或在生產過程中或
       production for such sale or in the form of materials or              提供服務時所耗用材料或物料形式持有的資
       supplies to be consumed in the production process or                 產。
       in the rendering of services.


       Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net                 存貨按成本與可變現淨值中的較低者入賬。
       realizable value.


       Cost is calculated using the weighted average cost                   成本按加權平均成本法計算，包括所有採購
       formula and comprises all costs of purchase, costs of                成本、加工成本及將存貨運至現時地點及達
       conversion and other costs incurred in bringing the                  致現狀產生的其他成本。就在製品而言，成
       inventories to their present location and condition.                 本包括直接勞工以及適當比例的間接成本
       In the case of work in progress, costs include direct               （根據正常運營能力計算）。
       labour and appropriate share of overheads based on
       normal operating capacity.


       Net realizable value is the estimated selling price in the           可變現淨值按日常業務過程中的估計售價減
       ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of              完成生產及銷售所需估計成本計算。
       completion and the estimated costs necessary to make
       the sale.

      it is probable that future taxable profits will be available           銷可動用資產時確認所有遞延稅項資產。可

      Future taxable profits that may support the recognition                產的日後應課稅利潤，包括撥回現有應課稅
      of deferred tax assets arising from deductible                         暫時差額將產生者，惟該等差額須與相同稅
      temporary differences include those that will arise from               務機關及相同應課稅實體有關，並預期在可
      the reversal of existing taxable temporary differences,                扣稅暫時差額預計撥回的同一期間或遞延稅
      provided those differences relate to the same taxation                 項資產所產生稅項損失可撥回或結轉的期間
      authority and the same taxable entity, and are expected                撥回。倘該等差額與相同稅務機關及相同應
      to reverse either in the same period as the expected                   課稅實體有關，並預期可於動用稅項損失或
      reversal of the deductible temporary difference or in                  抵免的一個或多個期間撥回，則釐定現有應

      criteria are adopted when determining whether existing                 相同準則。
      taxable temporary differences support the recognition

      and credits, that is, those differences are taken into

      that affect neither accounting nor taxable profit                      的一部分）的暫時性差異，以及有關投資附

(u)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                        (u)    撥備及或有負債

      Provisions are recognized when the Group has a                      當本集團須就過往事件承擔法律或推定責
      legal or constructive obligation arising as a result of a           任，而履行該責任很有可能導致經濟利益流
      past event, it is probable that an outflow of economic              出，且有關金額能夠可靠地估計時，則須確
      benefits will be required to settle the obligation and a            認撥備。對於貨幣時間價值影響重大的，撥
      reliable estimate can be made. Where the time value                 備以預計履行該責任的開支現值列賬。
      of money is material, provisions are stated at the
      present value of the expenditures expected to settle the
      obligation.


      Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                如果經濟利益不大可能流出，或無法對有關
      benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                  數額作出可靠估計，則會將該責任披露為或
      estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                有負債，除非經濟利益流出的可能性極微。
      contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow             其存在僅能以一宗或多宗未來事件的發生與
      of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,               否來確定的潛在責任，除非經濟利益流出的
      whose existence will only be confirmed by the                       可能性極微，否則亦披露為或有負債。
      occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future
      events, are also disclosed as contingent liabilities
      unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is
      remote.


      Where some or all of the expenditure required to settle             倘負債計提準備的部分或全部支出預計將由
      a provision is expected to be reimbursed by another                 另一方償還，則針對幾乎確定的預期償還確
      party, a separate asset is recognized for any expected              認一項單獨的資產。確認的報帳金額僅限於
      reimbursement that would be virtually certain. The                  負債計提準備的賬面金額。
      amount recognized for the reimbursement is limited to
      the carrying amount of the provision.

     from the date of issuance. Historically, the Group had            出現信貸虧損的情況。
     experienced no credit losses on bills receivable.

      At December 31, 2022, the Group did not recognize                於2022 年12 月31 日，本集團並無就本集團
      deferred tax liabilities of USD14,653,000 (2021:                 附屬公司的可分派利潤111,994,000 美元

      the Group’s subsidiaries amounted to USD111,994,000              負債14,653,000美元（2021年：12,167,000
      (2021: USD115,946,000), respectively. As the Group               美元）。由於本集團控制與該等附屬公司的
      controls the timing of the reversal of temporary                 未分派利潤有關的暫時差額撥回的時間，且

              statutory reserves can be used to make good                          儲備結餘在轉換後不少於該實體註冊

 (e)      Capital management                                          (e)   資本管理

          The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital              本集團管理資本的首要目標乃保障本集團能
          are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a             夠持續經營，以便透過對與風險水平相應的
          going concern, so that it can continue to provide returns         產品及服務定價以及獲得合理成本的融資，
          for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,             繼續為股東創造回報及為其他持份者帶來利
          by pricing products and services commensurately with              益。
          the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a
          reasonable cost.


          The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages              本集團定期積極對資本架構進行檢討及管
          its capital structure to maintaining a balance between            理，以在較高股東回報情況下可能伴隨的較
          the higher shareholders returns that might be possible            高借貸水平與良好資本狀況帶來的好處及保
          with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages               障之間取得平衡，並根據經濟狀況的變化對
          and security afforded by a sound capital position, and            資本架構作出調整。
          makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
          changes in economic conditions.


          The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of          本集團利用淨資本負債比率監察其資本架
          a net gearing ratio. For this purpose, the Group defines          構。就此而言，本集團將淨負債界定為流動
          net debt as total current and non-current bank loans              及非流動銀行貸款及租賃負債總額減現金及
          and lease liabilities less cash and cash equivalents              現金等價物及已抵押存款。本集團將資本界
          and pledged deposits. The Group defines capital as                定為包括所有權益組成部分。
          including all components of equity.

          Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency           本集團業務面臨正常業務過程中產生的信
          risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s                    貸、流動性、利率及貨幣風險。
          business.

          The Group’s exposure to these risks and financial risk              本集團面臨的該等風險及本集團為管理該等

          to manage these risks are described below:                          如下：

 (a)      Credit risk                                                   (a)   信貸風險
          Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will             信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務而導致本
          default on its contractual obligations resulting in a               集團產生財務虧損的風險。本集團信貸風險
          financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is             主要歸因於貿易及其他應收款項。本集團面
          primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.              對來自現金及現金等價物、已抵押存款及應
          The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash               收票據的信貸風險有限，原因是對手方為信
          and cash equivalents, pledged deposits and bills                    譽狀況良好的知名金融機構，本集團認為其
          receivable is limited because the counterparties are                信貸風險較低。
          reputable financial institutions with high credit standing,
          for which the Group considers to have low credit risk.

          Except for the financial guarantees given by the Group              除附註35所載由本集團提供的財務擔保外，
          as set out in Note 35 the Group does not provide any                本集團並無任何其他將令本集團承受信貸風
          other guarantees which would expose the Group to                    險的擔保。於報告期末有關該等財務擔保的
          credit risk. The maximum exposure to credit risk in                 最高信貸風險敞口乃披露於附註35。
          respect of these financial guarantees at the end of the
          reporting period is disclosed in Note 35.

          Trade receivables                                                   貿易應收款項

          The Group has established a credit risk management                  本集團已制定信貸風險管理政策，據此對所
          policy under which individual credit evaluations are                有需要超過一定金額的信貸的客戶均須進行
          performed on all customers requiring credit over                    個別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往到
          a certain amount. These evaluations focus on the                    期作出付款的記錄及目前的支付能力，並計
          customer’s past history of making payments when                     及客戶的特定資料以及有關客戶經營所在經
          due and current ability to pay and take into account                濟環境的資料。貿易應收款項自賬單日期起
          information specific to the customer as well as                     計30 至180 日內到期。正常情況下，本集團
          pertaining to the economic environment in which the                 不會收取客戶的抵押品。
          customer operates. Trade receivables are due within
          30 to 180 days from the date of billing. Normally, the
          Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

          The Group has no significant concentration of credit                本集團於客戶營運所屬的行業或所在的國家
          risk in industries or countries in which the customers              並無重大信貸集中風險。重大信貸集中風險
          operate. Significant concentrations of credit risk                  主要由於本集團與個別客戶往來時須承受重
          primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure             大風險所致。於2022 年12 月31 日，貿易應
          to individual customers. As at December 31, 2022,                   收款項總額中有32%（2021 年：30%）應收

          due from the Group’s largest customer and 68% (2021:                年：68%）應收自本集團的五名最大客戶。

(b)   Liquidity risk                                               (b)    流動性風險

      Individual operating entities within the Group are                  本集團旗下個別經營實體負責各自現金管
      responsible for their own cash management, including                理，包括現金盈餘短期投資及為滿足預期現
      the short-term investment of cash surpluses and the                 金要求而籌集貸款，惟借款超出一定授權預
      raising of loans to cover expected cash demands,                    定水平後須取得母公司董事會批准。本集團
      subject to approval by the parent company’s board                   的政策為定期監察其流動資金需求及其遵守
      when the borrowings exceed certain predetermined                    借貸契諾的情況，以確保維持充足的現金儲
      levels of authority. The Group’s policy is to regularly             備及來自主要金融機構的足夠承諾額度以滿
      monitor its liquidity requirements and its compliance               足其短期及長期的流動資金需求。
      with lending covenants to ensure that it maintains
      sufficient reserves of cash and adequate committed
      lines of funding from major financial institutions to meet
      its liquidity requirements in the short and longer term.
